============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

   195	function PhonePreview({
   196	  title,
   197	  metric,
   198	  variant,
   199	  href,
   200	  screenImageSrc,
   201	  screenImageAlt,
   202	}: {
   203	  href?: string;
   204	  title: string;
   205	  metric: string;
   206	  variant: "commerce" | "finance";
   207	  screenImageSrc?: string;
   208	  screenImageAlt?: string;
   209	}) {
   210	  const className = `phone-shell phone-shell-${variant}${
   211	    screenImageSrc ? " phone-shell-capture" : ""
   212	  }${href ? " phone-shell-link" : ""}`;
   213	  const content = (
   214	    <>
   215	      <div className="phone-speaker" />
   216	      <div className={`phone-screen${screenImageSrc ? " has-image" : ""}`}>
   217	        {screenImageSrc ? (
   218	          <div className="phone-screen-image-frame">
   219	            <Image
   220	              alt={screenImageAlt ?? `${title} app screen`}
   221	              className="phone-screen-image"
   222	              fill
   223	              sizes="245px"
   224	              src={screenImageSrc}
   225	            />
   226	          </div>
   227	        ) : (
   228	          <>
   229	            <div className="phone-status">
   230	              <span>{title}</span>
   231	              <span>5G</span>
   232	            </div>
   233	            <div className="phone-chart">
   234	              <div />
   235	              <div />
   236	              <div />
   237	              <div />
   238	            </div>
   239	            <div className="phone-cardline wide" />
   240	            <div className="phone-cardline" />
   241	            <div className="phone-grid">
   242	              <span />
   243	              <span />
   244	              <span />
   245	              <span />
   246	            </div>
   247	            <div className="phone-cta">{metric}</div>
   248	          </>
   249	        )}
   250	      </div>
   251	    </>
   252	  );
   253	
   254	  if (href) {
   255	    return (
   256	      <a
   257	        aria-label={`Open ${title} case study`}
   258	        className={className}
   259	        href={href}
   260	      >
   261	        {content}
   262	      </a>
   263	    );
   264	  }
   265	
   266	  return <div className={className}>{content}</div>;
   267	}

    74	function getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset() {
    75	  const isWideLandscape = window.matchMedia(
    76	    "(min-width: 1024px) and (orientation: landscape)",
    77	  ).matches;
    78	  const isPortrait = window.matchMedia("(orientation: portrait)").matches;
    79	
    80	  if (isWideLandscape) {
    81	    return Math.max(18, Math.min(44, window.innerHeight * 0.035));
    82	  }
    83	
    84	  if (isPortrait) {
    85	    return Math.max(14, Math.min(36, window.innerHeight * 0.03));
    86	  }
    87	
    88	  return Math.max(16, Math.min(40, window.innerHeight * 0.0325));
    89	}
    90	
    91	function scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(caseStudyId: string) {
    92	  const targetCard = document.getElementById(caseStudyId);
    93	
    94	  if (!targetCard) {
    95	    return;
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  const caseGrid = targetCard.closest<HTMLElement>(".case-grid");
    99	  const targetTop = targetCard.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   100	  const rowCards = caseGrid
   101	    ? Array.from(caseGrid.children).filter((child): child is HTMLElement => {
   102	        if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement)) {
   103	          return false;
   104	        }
   105	
   106	        const cardTop = child.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   107	
   108	        return Math.abs(cardTop - targetTop) <= 8;
   109	      })
   110	    : [targetCard];
   111	  const rowTop = Math.min(
   112	    ...rowCards.map((card) => card.getBoundingClientRect().top),
   113	  );
   114	
   115	  window.scrollTo({
   116	    behavior: "smooth",
   117	    top: window.scrollY + rowTop - getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset(),
   118	  });
   119	
   120	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", `#${caseStudyId}`);
   121	}

   382	export function ProjectDeviceStack({
   383	  assetGap,
   384	  color,
   385	  hasTablet,
   386	  hasWeb,
   387	  mobilePlatforms,
   388	  responsiveAssetGap,
   389	  showPlatformLabels,
   390	  webIconScale,
   391	}: {
   392	  assetGap?: string;
   393	  color: string;
   394	  hasTablet?: boolean;
   395	  hasWeb?: boolean;
   396	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   397	  responsiveAssetGap?: ResponsiveAssetGap;
   398	  showPlatformLabels?: boolean;
   399	  webIconScale?: number;
   400	}) {
   401	  const stackRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
   402	  const [measuredAssetGap, setMeasuredAssetGap] = useState<string | null>(null);
   403	  const stackStyle: CSSProperties & {
   404	    "--project-device-stack-gap"?: string;
   405	  } = {
   406	    color,
   407	  };
   408	  const stackGap = measuredAssetGap ?? assetGap;
   409	
   410	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   411	    if (!responsiveAssetGap) {
   412	      setMeasuredAssetGap(null);
   413	      return;
   414	    }
   415	
   416	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   417	    const measurementTarget = stack?.parentElement ?? stack;
   418	
   419	    if (!measurementTarget) {
   420	      return;
   421	    }
   422	
   423	    let frame = 0;
   424	    const updateGap = () => {
   425	      if (frame) {
   426	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   427	      }
   428	
   429	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   430	        const laneWidth = measurementTarget.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   431	        const activeRange = Math.max(
   432	          1,
   433	          responsiveAssetGap.wideLanePx - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx,
   434	        );
   435	        const progress = Math.min(
   436	          1,
   437	          Math.max(
   438	            0,
   439	            (laneWidth - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx) / activeRange,
   440	          ),
   441	        );
   442	        const nextGap =
   443	          responsiveAssetGap.minPx +
   444	          (responsiveAssetGap.maxPx - responsiveAssetGap.minPx) * progress;
   445	        const nextGapValue = `${nextGap.toFixed(2)}px`;
   446	
   447	        setMeasuredAssetGap((currentGap) =>
   448	          currentGap === nextGapValue ? currentGap : nextGapValue,
   449	        );
   450	      });
   451	    };
   452	
   453	    updateGap();
   454	
   455	    const resizeObserver =
   456	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   457	        ? null
   458	        : new ResizeObserver(updateGap);
   459	
   460	    resizeObserver?.observe(measurementTarget);
   461	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   462	
   463	    return () => {
   464	      if (frame) {
   465	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   466	      }
   467	
   468	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   469	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   470	    };
   471	  }, [
   472	    responsiveAssetGap?.maxPx,
   473	    responsiveAssetGap?.minPx,
   474	    responsiveAssetGap?.tightLanePx,
   475	    responsiveAssetGap?.wideLanePx,
   476	  ]);
   477	
   478	  if (stackGap) {
   479	    stackStyle["--project-device-stack-gap"] = stackGap;
   480	  }
   481	
   482	  if (showPlatformLabels) {
   483	    const platforms: Array<MobilePlatform | "web"> = [];
   484	    const factorDevices: Array<"phone" | "tablet"> = [];
   485	
   486	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("android") || hasTablet) {
   487	      platforms.push("android");
   488	    }
   489	
   490	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("apple")) {
   491	      platforms.push("apple");
   492	    }
   493	
   494	    if (hasWeb) {
   495	      platforms.push("web");
   496	    }
   497	
   498	    if (mobilePlatforms.length > 0) {
   499	      factorDevices.push("phone");
   500	    }
   501	
   502	    if (hasTablet) {
   503	      factorDevices.push("tablet");
   504	    }
   505	
   506	    return (
   507	      <span
   508	        className="carousel-button-project case-card-title-platform-list"
   509	        ref={stackRef}
   510	        style={stackStyle}
   511	      >
   512	        <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-platform">
   513	          PLATFORM
   514	        </span>
   515	        <span className="case-card-title-platform-divider" aria-hidden="true" />
   516	        <span className="case-card-title-platform-items case-card-title-platform-items-platform">
   517	          {platforms.map((platform) => (
   518	            <PlatformIconSlot
   519	              key={platform}
   520	              label={
   521	                platform === "web"
   522	                  ? "WEB"
   523	                  : platform === "android"
   524	                    ? "AND"
   525	                    : "IOS"
   526	              }
   527	            />
   528	          ))}
   529	        </span>
   530	        {factorDevices.length > 0 ? (
   531	          <>
   532	            <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-factor">
   533	              DEVICE
   534	            </span>
   535	            <span className="case-card-title-platform-items case-card-title-platform-items-factor">
   536	              {factorDevices.map((device) => (
   537	                <PlatformIconSlot
   538	                  key={device}
   539	                  label={device === "phone" ? "PHONE" : "TABLET"}
   540	                />
   541	              ))}
   542	            </span>
   543	          </>
   544	        ) : null}
   545	      </span>
   546	    );
   547	  }
   548	
   549	  return (
   550	    <span className="carousel-button-project" ref={stackRef} style={stackStyle}>
   551	      {mobilePlatforms.map((platform) => (
   552	        <PhoneMark key={platform} mobilePlatforms={[platform]} />
   553	      ))}
   554	      {hasTablet ? <TabletMark /> : null}
   555	      {hasWeb ? <WebMark scale={webIconScale} /> : null}
   556	    </span>
   557	  );
   558	}
   559	
   560	function CarouselArrowMark() {
   561	  return (
   562	    <img
   563	      alt=""
   564	      aria-hidden
   565	      className="carousel-button-arrow-mark"
   566	      draggable={false}
   567	      style={{
   568	        display: "block",
   569	        height: "100%",
   570	        verticalAlign: "top",
   571	        width: "100%",
   572	      }}
   573	      src="/images/carousel-arrow.svg"
   574	    />
   575	  );
   576	}
   577	
   578	export function ProjectCarouselButton() {
   579	  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
   580	  const projectButtonRef = useRef<HTMLAnchorElement>(null);
   581	  const activeProject = projectLinks[activeIndex];
   582	
   583	  const handleArrowClick = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
   584	    event.preventDefault();
   585	    scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(activeProject.caseStudyId);
   586	  };
   587	
   588	  useEffect(() => {
   589	    const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
   590	      setActiveIndex((currentIndex) => (currentIndex + 1) % projectLinks.length);
   591	    }, 5000);
   592	
   593	    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
   594	  }, []);
   595	
   596	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   597	    let frame = 0;
   598	
   599	    const syncHeroActionGeometry = () => {
   600	      if (frame) {
   601	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   602	      }
   603	
   604	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   605	        const heroCopy = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-copy");
   606	        const heroActions =
   607	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
   608	        const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
   609	        const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
   610	        const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
   611	        const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
   612	        const profilePhoto =
   613	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
   614	        const socialButton =
   615	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-social-button");
   616	        const visualFrameSelectors = [
   617	          ".profile-photo",
   618	          ".phone-shell-commerce",
   619	          ".phone-shell-finance",
   620	          ".tablet-shell",
   621	        ];
   622	
   623	        if (proofPanel && profilePhoto) {
   624	          const proofRect = proofPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
   625	          const profileRect = profilePhoto.getBoundingClientRect();
   626	          const proofWidth = profileRect.right - proofRect.left;
   627	
   628	          if (proofWidth > 0) {
   629	            [heroCopy, proofPanel, stackRow].forEach((element) => {
   630	              element?.style.setProperty(
   631	                "--hero-proof-landscape-width",
   632	                `${proofWidth}px`,
   633	              );
   634	            });
   635	          }
   636	        }
   637	
   638	        if (heroVisual && navBar && stackRow) {
   639	          const isTwoColumnHero = window.matchMedia(
   640	            "(min-width: 981px)",
   641	          ).matches;
   642	
   643	          if (!isTwoColumnHero) {
   644	            heroVisual.style.removeProperty("--hero-visual-center-offset");
   645	          } else {
   646	            const visualRects = visualFrameSelectors
   647	              .map((selector) =>
   648	                document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector),
   649	              )
   650	              .filter((element): element is HTMLElement => Boolean(element))
   651	              .map((element) => element.getBoundingClientRect());
   652	
   653	            if (visualRects.length > 0) {
   654	              const navRect = navBar.getBoundingClientRect();
   655	              const stackRect = stackRow.getBoundingClientRect();
   656	              const visualTop = Math.min(
   657	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.top),
   658	              );
   659	              const visualBottom = Math.max(
   660	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.bottom),
   661	              );
   662	              const currentOffset =
   663	                Number.parseFloat(
   664	                  window
   665	                    .getComputedStyle(heroVisual)
   666	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-visual-center-offset"),
   667	                ) || 0;
   668	              const visualCenterWithoutOffset =
   669	                (visualTop + visualBottom) / 2 - currentOffset;
   670	              const targetCenter = (navRect.top + stackRect.bottom) / 2;
   671	              const nextOffset = targetCenter - visualCenterWithoutOffset;
   672	
   673	              heroVisual.style.setProperty(
   674	                "--hero-visual-center-offset",
   675	                `${nextOffset}px`,
   676	              );
   677	            }
   678	          }
   679	        }
   680	
   681	        if (heroActions && socialButton) {
   682	          const heroActionsWidth = heroActions.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   683	          const socialButtonHeight =
   684	            socialButton.getBoundingClientRect().height;
   685	
   686	          if (heroCopy && heroActionsWidth > 0) {
   687	            heroCopy.style.setProperty(
   688	              "--hero-text-stack-width",
   689	              `${heroActionsWidth}px`,
   690	            );
   691	          }
   692	
   693	          if (socialButtonHeight > 0) {
   694	            const setPx = (name: string, value: number) => {
   695	              heroActions.style.setProperty(name, `${value}px`);
   696	            };
   697	
   698	            setPx("--hero-social-button-height", socialButtonHeight);
   699	            setPx("--hero-social-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.295833);
   700	            setPx("--hero-social-icon-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.672);
   701	            const baseSocialButtonHeight = 48;
   702	            const baseSocialGap = baseSocialButtonHeight * 0.045833 + 1;
   703	            const stretchedSocialGap =
   704	              Math.max(0, socialButtonHeight - baseSocialButtonHeight) *
   705	              0.091666;
   706	
   707	            setPx("--hero-social-gap", baseSocialGap + stretchedSocialGap);
   708	            setPx("--hero-social-pad-x", socialButtonHeight / 8);
   709	            setPx("--hero-view-label-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.275);
   710	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
   711	              "--hero-eyebrow-font-size",
   712	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.367}px`,
   713	            );
   714	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
   715	              "--hero-lede-font-size",
   716	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.5}px`,
   717	            );
   718	            setPx(
   719	              "--hero-carousel-base-font-size",
   720	              socialButtonHeight * 0.34375,
   721	            );
   722	            setPx(
   723	              "--hero-carousel-name-font-size",
   724	              socialButtonHeight * 0.352917,
   725	            );
   726	            setPx(
   727	              "--hero-carousel-category-font-size",
   728	              socialButtonHeight * 0.176458,
   729	            );
   730	            setPx(
   731	              "--hero-carousel-arrow-font-size",
   732	              socialButtonHeight * 0.458333,
   733	            );
   734	          }
   735	        }
   736	
   737	        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("hero-stack-sync"));
   738	
   739	        const viewLabel =
   740	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
   741	        const githubText =
   742	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
   743	        const githubIcon =
   744	          document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
   745	
   746	        if (viewLabel && githubText && githubIcon) {
   747	          const viewLabelRect = viewLabel.getBoundingClientRect();
   748	          const githubTextRect = githubText.getBoundingClientRect();
   749	          const githubIconRect = githubIcon.getBoundingClientRect();
   750	          const clusterLeft = Math.min(githubTextRect.left, githubIconRect.left);
   751	          const stackLeft = clusterLeft - viewLabelRect.left;
   752	
   753	          viewLabel.style.setProperty(
   754	            "--hero-view-stack-left",
   755	            `${stackLeft}px`,
   756	          );
   757	        }
   758	      });
   759	    };
   760	
   761	    syncHeroActionGeometry();
   762	    window.addEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
   763	
   764	    const targetIcon =
   765	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".dribbble-icon-mark") ??
   766	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".linkedin-icon-mark");
   767	    const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
   768	    const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
   769	    const heroActions = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
   770	    const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
   771	    const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
   772	    const profilePhoto = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
   773	    const targetButton = targetIcon?.closest(".hero-social-button");
   774	    const viewLabel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
   775	    const githubText = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
   776	    const githubIcon =
   777	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
   778	    const githubButton = githubText?.closest(".github-button");
   779	    const resizeObserver =
   780	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   781	        ? null
   782	        : new ResizeObserver(syncHeroActionGeometry);
   783	
   784	    if (resizeObserver) {
   785	      if (projectButtonRef.current) {
   786	        resizeObserver.observe(projectButtonRef.current);
   787	      }
   788	
   789	      if (heroActions) {
   790	        resizeObserver.observe(heroActions);
   791	      }
   792	
   793	      if (navBar) {
   794	        resizeObserver.observe(navBar);
   795	      }
   796	
   797	      if (heroVisual) {
   798	        resizeObserver.observe(heroVisual);
   799	      }
   800	
   801	      if (stackRow) {
   802	        resizeObserver.observe(stackRow);
   803	      }
   804	
   805	      if (proofPanel) {
   806	        resizeObserver.observe(proofPanel);
   807	      }
   808	
   809	      if (profilePhoto) {
   810	        resizeObserver.observe(profilePhoto);
   811	      }
   812	
   813	      if (targetIcon) {
   814	        resizeObserver.observe(targetIcon);
   815	      }
   816	
   817	      if (targetButton) {
   818	        resizeObserver.observe(targetButton);
   819	      }
   820	
   821	      if (viewLabel) {
   822	        resizeObserver.observe(viewLabel);
   823	      }
   824	
   825	      if (githubText) {
   826	        resizeObserver.observe(githubText);
   827	      }
   828	
   829	      if (githubIcon) {
   830	        resizeObserver.observe(githubIcon);
   831	      }
   832	
   833	      if (githubButton) {
   834	        resizeObserver.observe(githubButton);
   835	      }
   836	    }
   837	
   838	    if ("fonts" in document) {
   839	      document.fonts.ready.then(syncHeroActionGeometry).catch(() => {});
   840	    }
   841	
   842	    return () => {
   843	      if (frame) {
   844	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   845	      }
   846	
   847	      window.removeEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
   848	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   849	    };
   850	  }, []);
   851	
   852	  return (
   853	    <span className="carousel-button-group">
   854	      <a
   855	        aria-label={`Open ${activeProject.name} project`}
   856	        className="button button-primary carousel-button"
   857	        href={activeProject.href}
   858	        ref={projectButtonRef}
   859	      >
   860	        <span className="carousel-button-window" aria-live="polite">
   861	          <span className="carousel-button-label" key={activeProject.href}>
   862	            <span
   863	              className={`carousel-button-action carousel-button-action-${activeProject.tone}`}
   864	              style={{
   865	                ...strokedWhiteTextStyle,
   866	                alignItems: "center",
   867	                color: projectNameColors[activeProject.tone],
   868	                display: "inline-flex",
   869	                flexDirection: "column",
   870	                fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-name-font-size)",
   871	                gap: "2.2px",
   872	                justifyContent: "center",
   873	                lineHeight: 0.95,
   874	              }}
   875	            >
   876	              <span
   877	                className="carousel-button-action-name"
   878	                style={{ fontSize: activeProject.nameFontSize }}
   879	              >
   880	                {activeProject.name}
   881	              </span>
   882	              <span
   883	                className="carousel-button-action-category"
   884	                style={{
   885	                  WebkitTextStroke: 0,
   886	                  color: "#000",
   887	                  fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-category-font-size)",
   888	                  fontWeight: 900,
   889	                  letterSpacing: "0.06em",
   890	                  lineHeight: 1,
   891	                  textAlign: "center",
   892	                  textShadow: "none",
   893	                }}
   894	              >
   895	                {activeProject.category}
   896	              </span>
   897	            </span>
   898	            <ProjectDeviceStack
   899	              color={projectNameColors[activeProject.tone]}
   900	              hasTablet={activeProject.hasTablet}
   901	              hasWeb={activeProject.hasWeb}
   902	              mobilePlatforms={activeProject.mobilePlatforms}
   903	            />
   904	          </span>
   905	        </span>
   906	      </a>
   907	      <a
   908	        aria-label={`Scroll to ${activeProject.name} case study`}
   909	        className="button button-primary carousel-arrow-button"
   910	        href={`#${activeProject.caseStudyId}`}
   911	        onClick={handleArrowClick}
   912	      >
   913	        <span aria-hidden className="carousel-button-arrow">
   914	          <CarouselArrowMark />
   915	        </span>
   916	      </a>
   917	    </span>
   918	  );
   919	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Prahl.dev Portfolio Site
     2	
     3	Next.js + Tailwind CSS portfolio start page for Prahl.dev.
     4	
     5	## What is included
     6	
     7	- `app/page.tsx` - the homepage/start page.
     8	- `app/globals.css` - the green/black visual system and responsive layout.
     9	- `public/images/profile-placeholder.png` - generated AI profile placeholder; replace this with your own square photo later using the same filename.
    10	- `public/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png` - the original generated mockup reference.
    11	- `public/favicon.svg` - simple green/black monogram favicon.
    12	
    13	## Local development
    14	
    15	```sh
    16	npm install
    17	npm run dev
    18	```
    19	
    20	Open `http://localhost:3000`.
    21	
    22	## Production build
    23	
    24	For Vercel, Netlify, or another Next.js host:
    25	
    26	```sh
    27	npm run build
    28	```
    29	
    30	For a simple static host:
    31	
    32	```sh
    33	npm run build:static
    34	```
    35	
    36	Then upload the generated `out/` folder.
    37	
    38	## Replace the profile photo
    39	
    40	Put your real square profile photo at:
    41	
    42	```txt
    43	public/images/profile-placeholder.png
    44	```
    45	
    46	Keep the filename the same and rebuild.


============================================================
FILE: DEPLOYMENT.md
============================================================

     1	# Deployment Notes
     2	
     3	## Best Option: Vercel
     4	
     5	1. Upload this `portfolio-site` folder to a GitHub repo.
     6	2. Import the repo in Vercel.
     7	3. Use the default Next.js settings.
     8	4. Build command: `npm run build`.
     9	
    10	## Netlify
    11	
    12	1. Upload this folder to a GitHub repo.
    13	2. Connect the repo in Netlify.
    14	3. Build command: `npm run build`.
    15	4. Publish directory: `.next`.
    16	5. Enable Netlify's Next.js runtime if prompted.
    17	
    18	## Simple Static Hosting
    19	
    20	Use this when the host only accepts HTML/CSS/JS files:
    21	
    22	```sh
    23	npm install
    24	npm run build:static
    25	```
    26	
    27	Upload the contents of:
    28	
    29	```txt
    30	out/
    31	```
    32	
    33	This mode does not need a running Node.js server after upload.
    34	
    35	## Contact Button
    36	
    37	The contact button currently uses:
    38	
    39	```txt
    40	mailto:hello@example.com
    41	```
    42	
    43	Replace `hello@example.com` in `app/page.tsx` with your real email address.

============================================================
FILE: .github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml
============================================================

     1	name: Build static export for Hostinger
     2	
     3	on:
     4	  push:
     5	    branches: [main]
     6	  workflow_dispatch:
     7	
     8	permissions:
     9	  contents: write
    10	
    11	concurrency:
    12	  group: hostinger-static-export
    13	  cancel-in-progress: true
    14	
    15	jobs:
    16	  build-and-publish:
    17	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    18	    steps:
    19	      - name: Check out source
    20	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    21	
    22	      - name: Set up Node
    23	        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
    24	        with:
    25	          node-version: 22
    26	          cache: npm
    27	
    28	      - name: Install dependencies
    29	        run: npm ci
    30	
    31	      - name: Build static export
    32	        run: npm run build:static
    33	
    34	      - name: Publish static export branch
    35	        env:
    36	          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    37	        run: |
    38	          cd out
    39	          git init -b hostinger-deploy
    40	          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
    41	          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
    42	          git add -A
    43	          git commit -m "Deploy static export from ${GITHUB_SHA}"
    44	          git remote add origin "https://x-access-token:${GITHUB_TOKEN}@github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}.git"
    45	          git push --force origin hostinger-deploy

============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/portfolio_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Portfolio project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Portfolio Next.js PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you do not understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	10. For this Portfolio repo/website project, always ask me for the commit message before committing or pushing.
    28	
    29	
    30	============================================================
    31	PORTFOLIO PROJECT NOTES
    32	============================================================
    33	
    34	- Main source branch: main
    35	- Deployment source: GitHub Actions builds main into the hostinger-deploy branch
    36	- Hostinger root directory: public_html
    37	- Main stack represented by the website: React / React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, Kotlin
    38	- Public website assets live under public/
    39	- Run this script before starting a Portfolio session to refresh the project snapshot.
    40	
    41	
    42	============================================================
    43	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    44	============================================================
    45	
    46	This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    47	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    48	
    49	The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
    50	- .git
    51	- node_modules
    52	- .next
    53	- out
    54	- dist
    55	- build
    56	- .cache
    57	- .turbo
    58	- .npm
    59	- .pnpm-store
    60	- .yarn
    61	- .DS_Store
    62	- next-env.d.ts
    63	- *.tsbuildinfo
    64	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    65	- *.zip
    66	- .env and .env*.local
    67	
    68	Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    69	
    70	
    71	============================================================
    72	PROJECT FILE TREE
    73	============================================================
    74	
    75	EOF
    76	}
    77	
    78	print_file() {
    79	  local file="$1"
    80	
    81	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    82	    {
    83	      echo ""
    84	      echo ""
    85	      echo "============================================================"
    86	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    87	      echo "============================================================"
    88	      echo ""
    89	      nl -ba "$file"
    90	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    91	  fi
    92	}
    93	
    94	print_inventory_header() {
    95	  local title="$1"
    96	
    97	  {
    98	    echo ""
    99	    echo ""
   100	    echo "============================================================"
   101	    echo "$title"
   102	    echo "============================================================"
   103	    echo ""
   104	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   105	}
   106	
   107	print_file_inventory_line() {
   108	  local file="$1"
   109	  local size
   110	  local modified
   111	
   112	  size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   113	  modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   114	  printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   115	}
   116	
   117	print_dir_inventory() {
   118	  local title="$1"
   119	  local dir="$2"
   120	
   121	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   122	
   123	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
   124	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   125	    return
   126	  fi
   127	
   128	  find "$dir" -type f \
   129	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   130	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   131	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   132	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   133	  done
   134	}
   135	
   136	print_matching_inventory() {
   137	  local title="$1"
   138	  shift
   139	
   140	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   141	
   142	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" "$@" -type f \
   143	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git/*" \
   144	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules/*" \
   145	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next/*" \
   146	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out/*" \
   147	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   148	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   149	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   150	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   151	  done
   152	}
   153	
   154	append_git_context() {
   155	  {
   156	    echo ""
   157	    echo ""
   158	    echo "============================================================"
   159	    echo "GIT CONTEXT"
   160	    echo "============================================================"
   161	    echo ""
   162	    echo "Current branch:"
   163	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" branch --show-current 2>/dev/null || true
   164	    echo ""
   165	    echo "Status:"
   166	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" status --short --branch 2>/dev/null || true
   167	    echo ""
   168	    echo "Recent commits:"
   169	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" log --oneline --decorate -10 2>/dev/null || true
   170	    echo ""
   171	    echo "Remotes:"
   172	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" remote -v 2>/dev/null || true
   173	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   174	}
   175	
   176	write_snapshot() {
   177	  write_header
   178	
   179	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   180	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   181	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules" -prune -o \
   182	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next" -prune -o \
   183	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out" -prune -o \
   184	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/dist" -prune -o \
   185	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   186	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   187	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.turbo" -prune -o \
   188	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.npm" -prune -o \
   189	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.pnpm-store" -prune -o \
   190	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.yarn" -prune -o \
   191	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   192	    -name "next-env.d.ts" -prune -o \
   193	    -name "*.tsbuildinfo" -prune -o \
   194	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   195	    -name "*.zip" -prune -o \
   196	    -name ".env" -prune -o \
   197	    -name ".env*.local" -prune -o \
   198	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   199	
   200	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   201	
   202	
   203	============================================================
   204	FILE CONTENTS - APP SOURCE
   205	============================================================
   206	
   207	EOF
   208	
   209	  for file in \
   210	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/layout.tsx" \
   211	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/page.tsx" \
   212	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/ProjectCarouselButton.tsx" \
   213	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/globals.css"
   214	  do
   215	    print_file "$file"
   216	  done
   217	
   218	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   219	
   220	
   221	============================================================
   222	FILE CONTENTS - ROOT / TOOLING / DEPLOYMENT
   223	============================================================
   224	
   225	EOF
   226	
   227	  for file in \
   228	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   229	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   230	    "$PROJECT_DIR/DEPLOYMENT.md" \
   231	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package.json" \
   232	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package-lock.json" \
   233	    "$PROJECT_DIR/next.config.ts" \
   234	    "$PROJECT_DIR/postcss.config.mjs" \
   235	    "$PROJECT_DIR/tsconfig.json" \
   236	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml" \
   237	    "$PROJECT_DIR/public/favicon.svg" \
   238	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   239	  do
   240	    print_file "$file"
   241	  done
   242	
   243	  append_git_context
   244	
   245	  print_dir_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY - PUBLIC IMAGES" "$PROJECT_DIR/public/images"
   246	  print_matching_inventory "PDF WORKLOG INVENTORY" \( -name "portfolio_worklog_*.pdf" \)
   247	  print_matching_inventory "DEPLOYMENT ARCHIVE INVENTORY" \( -name "*.zip" \)
   248	
   249	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   250	
   251	
   252	============================================================
   253	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   254	============================================================
   255	
   256	The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
   257	- .git
   258	- node_modules
   259	- .next
   260	- out
   261	- dist
   262	- build
   263	- .cache
   264	- .turbo
   265	- .npm
   266	- .pnpm-store
   267	- .yarn
   268	- .DS_Store
   269	- next-env.d.ts
   270	- *.tsbuildinfo
   271	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   272	- *.zip
   273	- .env and .env*.local
   274	
   275	The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
   276	
   277	EOF
   278	}
   279	
   280	write_snapshot
   281	
   282	echo "Done."
   283	echo ""
   284	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   285	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   286	echo ""
   287	echo "Open it with:"
   288	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""
